- **sample-size-power-calculator**: Computes required sample size, achieved power, or minimum detectable effect and writes the justification. Use when planning a quantitative study. - **sample-size-power-planning**: Use when a task involves planning or reviewing a study's sample-size rationale from an explicit outcome, effect, error rates, and design assumptions to identify the research or operational question, target population or system, relevant version, sensitive data, and approval boundary before acting. Use current primary sources, produce a traceable artifact, and verify it against explicit criteria. Trigger for planning, analysis, review, or troubleshooting in this focused domain; do not execute external writes or clinical actions without authorization. ([Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills](https://github.com/Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/science-research/statistics-evidence/sample-size-power-planning) · ★3)
- **sample-size-rationale-helper**: Draft the sample size rationale section of a study protocol: inputs, assumptions, and what must be justified. Drafts for research and education; a qualified human must review before use. ([salikkhann/openmedskills](https://github.com/salikkhann/openmedskills/tree/HEAD/dist/agent-skills/sample-size-rationale-helper) · ★5)
- **sample-size-sap**: Use when a sample size must be calculated, explained or justified, power computed, a Statistical Analysis Plan (SAP) written, or a statistical test chosen for a clinical or epidemiological study. Triggers: "tính cỡ mẫu", "cỡ mẫu bao nhiêu", "n bao nhiêu là đủ", "công thức cỡ mẫu", "giải thích cỡ mẫu cho hội đồng", "kế hoạch phân tích thống kê", "chọn test thống kê", "dùng test gì", "sample size", "power", "SAP", "effect size", "MCID", "non-inferiority margin", "cluster ICC"; or when a protocol lacks a sample-size section. ([drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra](https://github.com/drtranquanghung91-vn/clinical-research-orchestra/tree/HEAD/plugins/orchestra/skills/sample-size-sap) · ★2)
- **sample-size-scaling-curve-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify how a data-processing tool's CPU time and memory scale with sample number, or to benchmark one metabolomics pipeline against another (e.g. asari vs XCMS) on identical subsets of a public dataset. - **sample-size-weighted-effect-combination**: Use when the same feature pairs have Pearson correlation coefficients measured in multiple studies, each with its own sample size, and a single combined fixed/random-effects estimate per pair is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/sample-size-weighted-effect-combination))
- **sample-size-weighted-meta-analysis**: Use when contributing studies report only aggregate statistics (p-value, fold-change, sample size) per compound and you need to combine them into a single combined p-value, fold-change, and regulation direction per compound.
